- **differential-lipid-abundance-analysis**: Use when a targeted lipidomics dataset (Skyline CSV export or numerical matrix with sample-annotation table) must be statistically compared across clinical groups, e.g. HighFat_water vs NormalDiet_water or Cancer vs Benign, with or without confounder correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-lipid-abundance-analysis--7414cb05d11ec995))
- **differential-lipid-abundance-analysis**: Use when the input is a lipid feature (intensity) matrix plus a phenotype file and the goal is differential abundance and enrichment across lipid classes, chain length, and degree of saturation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-lipid-abundance-analysis--365ee40b5eee5b6a))
- **differential-lipid-abundance-testing**: Use when a normalized lipid abundance matrix (from LipidSearch/LIQUID output or Metabolomics Workbench import) is available and the goal is to find differentially abundant lipids between groups defined in a target-file metadata design, optionally with covariates or batch correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-lipid-abundance-testing))
- **differential-lipid-expression-analysis**: Use when when you have a LipidomicsExperiment object with logged and normalized Area measurements, sample grouping metadata (e.g., SampleType, Stage, Race), and a research question about whether specific lipid molecules or classes differ significantly between two or more sample groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/differential-lipid-expression-analysis))
- **differential-lipidomics-results-interpretation**: Use when you have a two-group (or multi-group) differential lipidomics comparison and want to know which lipid classes or chain attributes are preferentially enriched among the regulated lipids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-lipidomics-results-interpretation))
- **differential-marker-expression-testing**: Use when clustering results (e.g. 19 scCAN clusters on the Brain 1.3M dataset, GSE93421) lack true cell type labels and must be biologically validated and mapped to known cell types via marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-marker-expression-testing))
- **differential-metabolite-abundance-analysis**: Use when you have raw metabolomics data structured as a matrix with metabolites as rows and samples as columns, samples are annotated with group labels (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/differential-metabolite-abundance-analysis))
- **differential-metabolite-abundance-analysis**: Use when joint spatial transcriptomics–metabolomics data have been integrated and clustered by SpatialMETA and you need cluster-defining genes/metabolites plus metabolite pathway enrichment for one sub-cluster vs. the rest. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-abundance-analysis))
- **differential-metabolite-abundance-testing**: Use when a pixel x m/z feature matrix (e.g., 14,260 pixels x 3,044 ions from AFAD-ESI positive-mode mouse brain IMS at 100 um) has region assignments (auto-clusters or manual ROIs) and you need up/downregulated differential metabolites between two regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-abundance-testing--8274b5843fb2ecb1))
- **differential-metabolite-abundance-testing**: Use when you have matrices normalized by different strategies (tGAM/rGAM/rLOESS, QC-RLSC, SERFF, TIGER) and need to quantify how the choice of normalization changes the differential abundance hit set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-abundance-testing--b3a1b06b60354643))
- **differential-metabolite-abundance-testing**: Use when you have preprocessed and normalized metabolite measurements from two or more distinct biological groups (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/differential-metabolite-abundance-testing))
- **differential-metabolite-annotation-counting**: Use when a spatial metabolomics feature matrix (pixels × m/z ion peaks) has been pixel-clustered (e.g., UMAP-kmeans) and you must identify metabolites significantly upregulated in one anatomical region versus another, with per-region annotation counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-annotation-counting))
- **differential-metabolite-comparison-between-maize-varieties**: Use when comparing two maize root DESI-MSI datasets whose mass signatures were already extracted along a linear developmental gradient by DIMPLE and you need a conserved/unique partition of pooled m/z peaks per variety. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-comparison-between-maize-varieties))
- **differential-metabolite-detection**: Use when you have normalized and aligned lipidomic and metabolomic spectral features from the Multi-ABLE method across multiple biological samples grouped by phenotype (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/differential-metabolite-detection) · 2 copies)
- **differential-metabolite-detection-across-imputation-methods**: Decision support for whether missing-value imputation choices materially change the differential-metabolite lists and enriched pathways in an LC-MS untargeted metabolomics feature table, e.g. when a case study applied only one imputation method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-detection-across-imputation-methods))
- **differential-metabolite-feature-detection**: Use when raw MS1 LC-HRMS data from paired treated vs. heat-inactivated control incubations (e.g., 1,021 pesticides in human liver S9) must be feature-detected, aligned, and differentially screened to feed reaction-guided metabolite annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-feature-detection))
- **differential-metabolomics-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `Differential metabolomics` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. ([ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry](https://github.com/ma-compbio-lab/SkillFoundry/tree/HEAD/skills/metabolomics-and-other-omics/differential-metabolomics-starter) · ★46)
- **differential-methylated-region-detection**: Use when you have haplotype-resolved (hp1/hp2) site-level methylation frequencies from phased long reads and need to call DMRs for allele-specific methylation, or to benchmark against BS-seq/nanopore ASM calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/differential-methylated-region-detection))
- **differential-methylation**: This skill performs differential DNA methylation analysis (DMRs and DMCs) between experimental conditions using WGBS methylation tracks (BED/BedGraph). It standardizes input files into per-sample four-column Metilene tables, constructs a merged methylation matrix, runs Metilene for DMR detection, filters the results, and generates quick visualizations. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/tools/differential-methylation-majiayu000) · ★67 · 4 copies)
- **differential-methylation-calling**: Use when raw IDAT files plus a sample sheet from an Illumina 450K/EPIC methylation array study (e.g. from GEO) need differential methylation calling between groups, and a GUI-driven, reproducible pipeline is acceptable. - **differential-methylation-classification**: Use when PCR-bias-corrected (MethylCal or Moskalev's CPR) methylation levels for case/control pyrosequencing or bisulfite amplicon data must be called as gain/loss of methylation against a healthy-control ±3SD interval, e.g. BWS KCNQ1OT1 and H19/IGF2 calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-methylation-classification))
- **differential-methylation-region-testing**: Use when Bismark-aligned MethylC-seq (bisulfite) data in SAM/BAM format needs post-alignment processing to identify differentially methylated regions (DMRs) between two or more sample groups, e.g., IMR90 vs H1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-methylation-region-testing))
- **differential-methylation-results-filtering**: Use when you have a DMC results table from Illumina 450K/EPIC methylation analysis and need to restrict CpGs to promoter/5'UTR features or apply adjusted-P/logFC thresholds before visualization or enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-methylation-results-filtering))

- **differential-mutation-frequency-analysis**: Use when you have a TCGA-style MAF mutation file and sample groups defined by a continuous factor (e.g. top/bottom hypoxia GSVA score) and need per-gene mutation p-values and FDR between groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-mutation-frequency-analysis))
- **differential-network-edge-analysis**: Use when you have single-cell count matrices from two matched conditions (e.g., 211 normal vs 656 tumor immune cells from GSE114727) and want to detect co-expression edges present in one condition but absent in the other. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-network-edge-analysis))
- **differential-network-edge-count-comparison**: Use when a two-condition metabolomics network has been estimated under alternative lambda/regularization settings and you must tabulate total vs case-specific vs control-specific vs shared edges to judge tuning impact. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-network-edge-count-comparison))
- **differential-network-inference-boosted-trees**: Use when you have two tab-separated gene expression matrices (same genes, rows=genes, columns=samples) from two biological conditions and need a condition-specific differential network for downstream pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-network-inference-boosted-trees))
- **differential-network-inference-from-transcriptomics**: Use when two tab-separated gene expression matrices (genes as rows, first column named 'Gene') from two conditions are available and the goal is a condition-specific differential regulatory network with enriched disease-relevant pathways. - **differential-network-module-agreement-thresholding**: Use when a DNEA object with estimated two-condition partial correlation networks (e.g. TEDDY metabolomics, 134 metabolites) must be partitioned into metabolic modules and you need to set or adjust the consensus agreement threshold (tau). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-network-module-agreement-thresholding))
- **differential-pathway-activation-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters and candidate LR pairs are available and the goal is to determine which intracellular signaling pathways are significantly activated per cluster and which LR interactions are functionally associated with them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-pathway-activation-analysis))
- **differential-pathway-activity-prediction**: Use when you have spot-level (or cell-level) continuous feature values such as Seurat module scores plus 2D/3D spatial coordinates, and you need to rank gene sets or features by how non-randomly their activity is distributed across the tissue. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-pathway-activity-prediction))
- **differential-pathway-activity-t-testing**: Use when comparing two sample groups (e.g., DM2 vs NGT microarray profiles) to find pathways whose PLAGE-derived activity levels differ significantly, using t statistics calibrated by max-t label permutations. - **differential-pathway-activity-testing**: Use when single-cell transcriptomic data has been embedded in VEGA's pathway-informed latent space and you need to test which gene modules differ in activity between defined cell groups or conditions. - **differential-pathway-activity-testing-between-condition-groups**: Use when a normalized RNA-seq expression matrix with two condition groups (e.g., CF vs non-CF, or treated vs control) needs a benchmarked differential pathway activity comparison across rROMA and sample-wise scoring methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-pathway-activity-testing-between-condition-groups))
- **differential-pathway-ranking-comparison**: Use when comparing the computational efficiency (runtime, memory) and sensitivity of pathway activity scoring / differential pathway methods (scPAFA vs SCPA) on million-cell-scale scRNA-seq data with &gt;1000 pathways, or when assessing whether downsampling-based tools lose disease-relevant pathway. - **differential-peak-abundance-detection**: Use when after raw mass spectrometry data has been converted to a peak feature table (CSV or tabular format) containing mass-to-charge ratios, retention times, and intensity values across samples, and you need to reduce noise and identify which peaks show meaningful differential patterns between. - **differential-peak-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a fitted MSI segmentation exists (e.g., spatial shrunken centroids in Cardinal on imzML-derived pig fetus liver/heart data) and you must report which peaks each segment differentially enriches or lacks. - **differential-peak-motif-verification**: Use when two condition-specific differentially bound peak sets (e.g. female vs male GBM CC peaks) exist and the goal is to identify which TF motif families are enriched in one condition but not the other, reproducing published family-level enrichment patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-peak-motif-verification))
- **differential-pr-significance-testing**: Use when per-bin m6A scores from Nanopore dRNA-seq tools are already computed and you need to quantify whether restricting the precision-recall analysis to motif-containing, exclusion-zone-free, or high-coverage bins significantly improves tool performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/differential-pr-significance-testing))
- **differential-privacy**: Use when an analytic release or machine-learning pipeline needs a formal differential-privacy guarantee against inference about individual records; define adjacency, budget, mechanism, composition, utility, and the exact release claim.
